benchmarks/tts-vi/tts_vi_bench/engines/base.py
    1 """TtsEngine base — the contract every benchmarked Vietnamese TTS engine implements.
    2 
    3 Two additions over the English harness's version, each forced by what this
    4 comparison has to measure:
    5 
    6 - `synthesize` takes a **voice**, because each engine is measured on two of them
    7   rather than on one fixed speaker.
    8 - `synthesize_stream` exists at all, because one engine streams and the other
    9   does not, and time-to-first-audio is the metric most likely to decide the
   10   result. The base declares streaming unsupported so the engine that lacks it
   11   says so by inheriting rather than by raising.
   12 """
   13 
   14 from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
   15 from collections.abc import Iterator
   16 
   17 import numpy as np
   18 
   19 
   20 class TtsEngine(ABC):
   21     """One benchmarked Vietnamese TTS engine, at one voice."""
   22 
   23     engine_id: str
   24     #: gender -> voice token. The pair each engine speaks with by default, which
   25     #: is what `services/local-tts` exposes to callers through `POST /synthesize`.
   26     VOICES: dict[str, str]
   27     #: Whether `synthesize_stream` is real. False here; overridden where true.
   28     supports_streaming: bool = False
   29 
   30     @abstractmethod
   31     def load(self) -> None:
   32         """Load model weights. Timed separately from synthesis."""
   33 
   34     @abstractmethod
   35     def synthesize(self, text: str, voice: str) -> tuple[np.ndarray, int]:
   36         """Synthesize one sentence → (float32 samples in [-1, 1], sample_rate)."""
   37 
   38     def synthesize_stream(self, text: str, voice: str) -> Iterator[np.ndarray]:
   39         """Yield audio chunks as they are generated.
   40 
   41         The default is not a stub that returns nothing — it raises, so a caller
   42         that reaches it has a bug rather than a silently empty measurement.
   43         Check `supports_streaming` first.
   44         """
   45         raise NotImplementedError(f"{self.engine_id} does not stream")
   46 
   47     @abstractmethod
   48     def decode_params(self) -> dict:
   49         """Everything needed to reproduce this run, recorded in the result header.
   50 
   51         For a stochastic engine this includes the seed and every sampling
   52         parameter: without them a recorded number cannot be reproduced even on
   53         the same machine with the same weights.
   54         """
benchmarks/tts-vi/tts_vi_bench/engines/zerotts_vi.py
    1 """Vietnamese TTS — ZeroTTS (fp32 ONNX, CPU, torch-free).
    2 
    3 Every constructor argument below overrides a package default that would
    4 otherwise bias this benchmark. They are set explicitly, and the reasons are
    5 recorded here rather than in a commit message, because each one is invisible in
    6 the output it corrupts.
    7 
    8 **Threads.** `ZeroTTS.__init__` defaults `intra_op_num_threads=4`
    9 (`synthesizer.py`), where VieNeu is constructed with 8. Left alone the
   10 challenger runs on half the CPU of the incumbent and nothing in the result says
   11 so. `codec_intra_op_num_threads` is a second, separate knob — the codec decoder
   12 is its own session and the streaming path's cost lives there — so both are set
   13 and both are recorded.
   14 
   15 **Warm-up.** `__init__` defaults `warmup=True` and pushes a dummy request
   16 through every hot-path session during construction. That lands inside the timed
   17 `load()`, which VieNeu has no equivalent of, and the harness then runs its own
   18 untimed warm-up sentence on top — so ZeroTTS would warm twice and VieNeu once.
   19 Constructed with `warmup=False`; the harness's warm-up serves both engines
   20 identically.
   21 
   22 **Seeding.** The model draws from the *global* `np.random` on every generated
   23 frame and exposes no seed parameter. Unseeded, every latency, RTF, TTFA and WER
   24 figure is one draw from a distribution rather than a measurement. Seeded
   25 immediately before each synthesis, and the seed is recorded.
   26 
   27 Seeding is necessary but not sufficient for bit-reproducibility: the sampling
   28 happens *inside* the ONNX graph against fp32 logits, and top-k/top-p over an
   29 autoregressive loop turns a one-ULP difference into a divergent utterance. So
   30 the thread count and the `onnxruntime` version are pinned too, and results are
   31 claimed only at the recorded seed, threads and runtime.
   32 """
   33 
   34 from collections.abc import Iterator
   35 
   36 import numpy as np
   37 
   38 from ..measure import bench_threads
   39 from .base import TtsEngine
   40 
   41 #: The two presets this arm speaks with, chosen from the eight the package ships.
   42 #:
   43 #: Both are the manifest's "rõ ràng" (clear) pair — `baotrang` is labelled
   44 #: "trưởng thành, tin tức, rõ ràng, trung tính" and `quangminh` "trẻ, tin tức,
   45 #: rõ ràng, dứt khoát". That register is the one this product needs: a voice
   46 #: translator reads back short conversational utterances where being understood
   47 #: is the whole job. The rejected six carry prosody built for something else —
   48 #: four are "kể chuyện" (storytelling, long-form pacing), `hamy` is "hoạt hình"
   49 #: (cartoon, high and expressive), `tiendat` is "bình luận ... năng lượng cao"
   50 #: (commentary). See `scripts/audition_voices.py`, which renders all eight.
   51 DEFAULTS = {"female": "baotrang", "male": "quangminh"}
   52 
   53 #: Fixed so a rerun reproduces the run. Arbitrary value, deliberately recorded.
   54 SEED = 20260914
   55 
   56 
   57 class ZeroTtsVi(TtsEngine):
   58     engine_id = "zerotts-vi"
   59     VOICES = DEFAULTS
   60     supports_streaming = True
   61 
   62     def __init__(self) -> None:
   63         self._tts = None
   64         self._threads = bench_threads()
   65 
   66     def load(self) -> None:
   67         from zerotts import ZeroTTS
   68         from zerotts import hub
   69 
   70         self._revision = hub.DEFAULT_REVISION
   71         self._repo_id = hub.DEFAULT_REPO_ID
   72         self._tts = ZeroTTS.from_pretrained(
   73             hub.DEFAULT_REPO_ID,
   74             # The package pins its own weights revision to an exact commit sha,
   75             # with a docstring explaining that resolving `main` instead couples
   76             # every released version to whatever was last pushed. Passing None
   77             # takes that pin; it is a stronger guarantee than re-deriving one.
   78             revision=None,
   79             intra_op_num_threads=self._threads,
   80             codec_intra_op_num_threads=self._threads,
   81             warmup=False,
   82         )
   83 
   84     def synthesize(self, text: str, voice: str) -> tuple[np.ndarray, int]:
   85         np.random.seed(SEED)
   86         samples = self._tts.synthesize(text, voice=voice)
   87         return np.asarray(samples, dtype=np.float32).reshape(-1), self._tts.sample_rate
   88 
   89     def synthesize_stream(self, text: str, voice: str) -> Iterator[np.ndarray]:
   90         """Yield chunks, seeding at the point the draws actually happen.
   91 
   92         `synthesize_stream` is a generator function, so calling it runs nothing —
   93         not even voice resolution — and the RNG draws happen lazily, one per
   94         frame, as the consumer iterates. Seeding before the call rather than
   95         before iteration would leave the stream exposed to any other global-RNG
   96         consumer that ran in between. The seed is applied here, immediately
   97         before the first `next()`, and `decode_params` records that it was.
   98         """
   99         stream = self._tts.synthesize_stream(text, voice=voice)
  100         np.random.seed(SEED)
  101         for chunk in stream:
  102             yield np.asarray(chunk, dtype=np.float32).reshape(-1)
  103 
  104     @property
  105     def sample_rate(self) -> int:
  106         return self._tts.sample_rate
  107 
  108     def decode_params(self) -> dict:
  109         import importlib.metadata as md
  110 
  111         return {
  112             "package": "zerotts",
  113             "package_version": md.version("zerotts"),
  114             "repo_id": self._repo_id,
  115             "weights_revision": self._revision,
  116             "model_dir": str(getattr(self._tts, "model_dir", "")),
  117             "intra_op_num_threads": self._threads,
  118             "codec_intra_op_num_threads": self._threads,
  119             "warmup_on_construct": False,
  120             "seed": SEED,
  121             "seed_applied_at": "immediately before iteration (stream) / call (whole)",
  122             # The package's own defaults, recorded because they are what was run.
  123             "cfg_scale": 1.0,
  124             "text_temperature": 1.0,
  125             "text_topk": 50,
  126             "audio_temperature": 0.8,
  127             "audio_topk": 25,
  128             "audio_topp": 0.95,
  129             "audio_repetition_penalty": 1.2,
  130         }
benchmarks/tts-vi/tts_vi_bench/engines/vieneu_vi.py
    1 """Vietnamese TTS — VieNeu v3 Turbo (ONNX/CPU, torch-free). The incumbent.
    2 
    3 Constructed exactly as `services/local-tts/engines/vieneu_vi.py` constructs it,
    4 because the point of this arm is to measure what the service actually ships. The
    5 `fp32` pin is the part most easily lost: the package defaults to an int8 backbone
    6 graph, and the sidecar records that fp32 is the graph its two voices were
    7 auditioned on. Measuring int8 here would measure something no user hears.
    8 
    9 No streaming. That is not an omission in this file — the package exposes no
   10 streaming API, which is itself one of the findings this benchmark reports, and
   11 the TTFA arms in `run_engine` handle it by measuring clause-split synthesis
   12 instead of leaving a blank.
   13 """
   14 
   15 import numpy as np
   16 
   17 from ..measure import bench_threads
   18 from .base import TtsEngine
   19 
   20 #: The two presets the sidecar speaks with, by gender. Both were chosen upstream
   21 #: by listening to every voice the model shipped.
   22 DEFAULTS = {"female": "Mai Anh", "male": "Thanh Bình"}
   23 
   24 
   25 class VieNeuVi(TtsEngine):
   26     engine_id = "vieneu-vi"
   27     VOICES = DEFAULTS
   28     supports_streaming = False
   29 
   30     def __init__(self) -> None:
   31         self._engine = None
   32         self._threads = bench_threads()
   33 
   34     def load(self) -> None:
   35         from vieneu import Vieneu
   36 
   37         self._engine = Vieneu(
   38             mode="v3turbo",  # CPU -> torch-free ONNX
   39             precision="fp32",  # the graph the shipped voices were auditioned on
   40             threads=self._threads,
   41         )
   42 
   43     def synthesize(self, text: str, voice: str) -> tuple[np.ndarray, int]:
   44         samples = self._engine.infer(text, voice=str(voice))
   45         return np.asarray(samples, dtype=np.float32).reshape(-1), self._engine.sample_rate
   46 
   47     @property
   48     def sample_rate(self) -> int:
   49         return self._engine.sample_rate
   50 
   51     def decode_params(self) -> dict:
   52         import importlib.metadata as md
   53 
   54         return {
   55             "package": "vieneu",
   56             "package_version": md.version("vieneu"),
   57             "mode": "v3turbo",
   58             "precision": "fp32",
   59             "threads": self._threads,
   60             # Recorded for symmetry with the ZeroTTS header, where the answer is
   61             # not "none". VieNeu has no sampler, so a rerun is bit-identical.
   62             "seed": None,
   63             "stochastic": False,
   64         }
benchmarks/tts-vi/scripts/build_vivos_set.py
    1 """Emit the VIVOS comparability arm from the STT harness's manifest.
    2 
    3 Reads `benchmarks/stt/data/manifest-vi.jsonl` and never writes to it.
    4 
    5 Two things this script decides, both of which the report has to state:
    6 
    7 **Casing.** VIVOS references are ALL CAPS. ZeroTTS processes raw orthography
    8 with no grapheme-to-phoneme stage, so casing can reach the model — feeding it
    9 shouted text would measure something no caller produces. So `text` is
   10 sentence-cased for synthesis while `ref_text` keeps the corpus's original caps,
   11 and the scorer's normalizer (which lowercases both sides) reconciles them.
   12 
   13 **Why this arm is secondary.** ZeroBench-TTS redistributes VIVOS, so ZeroTTS may
   14 have seen this material. It is here for comparability with the existing STT
   15 numbers, which were measured on these exact 50 utterances; the conversational
   16 arm is the one the verdict rests on.
   17 """
   18 
   19 import json
   20 import sys
   21 from pathlib import Path
   22 
   23 BENCH_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
   24 SOURCE = BENCH_ROOT.parent / "stt" / "data" / "manifest-vi.jsonl"
   25 OUT = BENCH_ROOT / "data" / "sentences-vivos.jsonl"
   26 
   27 HEADER = """\
   28 # VIVOS — the comparability arm. Built by scripts/build_vivos_set.py; do not edit.
   29 #
   30 # Source: benchmarks/stt/data/manifest-vi.jsonl (read-only), the same 50
   31 # utterances the STT harness measured, which is what makes the human-speech
   32 # control floors in docs/development-journey.md apply here.
   33 #
   34 # CASING: `ref_text` keeps the corpus's ALL CAPS; `text` is sentence-cased for
   35 # synthesis, because ZeroTTS reads raw orthography with no G2P stage and no
   36 # caller ever sends shouted text. The scorer's normalizer lowercases both sides.
   37 #
   38 # CAVEAT: ZeroBench-TTS redistributes VIVOS, so ZeroTTS may have seen this
   39 # material. Secondary to the conversational arm for exactly that reason.
   40 """
   41 
   42 
   43 def sentence_case(text: str) -> str:
   44     """ALL CAPS -> sentence case, leaving Vietnamese diacritics untouched.
   45 
   46     `str.capitalize()` would be wrong: it lowercases the rest of the string but
   47     also strips any legitimate internal capital. These references carry no
   48     proper-noun casing to preserve (they are uniformly upper), so lowering the
   49     tail and raising the first character is exact here.
   50     """
   51     text = text.strip().lower()
   52     return text[:1].upper() + text[1:] if text else text
   53 
   54 
   55 def main() -> int:
   56     if not SOURCE.exists():
   57         print(f"[error] missing source manifest {SOURCE}", file=sys.stderr)
   58         return 1
   59 
   60     rows = []
   61     for line in SOURCE.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
   62         line = line.strip()
   63         if not line:
   64             continue
   65         src = json.loads(line)
   66         ref = src["ref_text"].strip()
   67         rows.append(
   68             {
   69                 # Prefixed so it cannot collide with a conversational id inside
   70                 # the shared results tree.
   71                 "id": f"vivos-{src['id']}",
   72                 "text": sentence_case(ref),
   73                 "ref_text": ref,
   74                 "tags": ["vivos"],
   75             }
   76         )
   77 
   78     OUT.write_text(
   79         HEADER + "\n".join(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) for r in rows) + "\n",
   80         encoding="utf-8",
   81     )
   82     print(f"[done] {len(rows)} rows -> {OUT.relative_to(BENCH_ROOT)}")
   83     return 0
   84 
   85 
   86 if __name__ == "__main__":
   87     sys.exit(main())
benchmarks/tts-vi/scripts/seed_sensitivity.py
    1 """How much does each engine's intelligibility move from one run to the next?
    2 
    3 The main benchmark scores each arm once per run tag. That is enough for latency,
    4 where the two tags bracket the spread — and NOT enough for WER, because both
    5 engines turn out to vary run to run for different reasons:
    6 
    7 - **ZeroTTS** samples from the global RNG every frame. Seeding pins it exactly,
    8   which is what makes the main run reproducible, but it also means the headline
    9   WER is ONE draw from a distribution. A different seed is a different draw.
   10 - **VieNeu** has no sampler and is still not byte-reproducible (0/41 identical
   11   across the two tags), so it has a spread of its own with no knob to set.
   12 
   13 If either spread is comparable to the gap between the engines, that gap is not a
   14 property of the engines and no verdict can rest on it. This script measures both
   15 spreads so they can be set beside the gap.
   16 
   17 Writes to `results/seed-sensitivity/`; touches nothing the main run produced.
   18 
   19     uv run python scripts/seed_sensitivity.py --engine zerotts-vi --voice baotrang --seeds 11,22,33
   20     uv run python scripts/seed_sensitivity.py --engine vieneu-vi --voice "Mai Anh" --runs 6
   21 """
   22 
   23 import argparse
   24 import json
   25 import sys
   26 from pathlib import Path
   27 
   28 import numpy as np
   29 import soundfile as sf
   30 
   31 sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent))
   32 
   33 from tts_vi_bench.asr_judges import PhoWhisperJudge  # noqa: E402
   34 from tts_vi_bench.engines.vieneu_vi import VieNeuVi  # noqa: E402
   35 from tts_vi_bench.engines.zerotts_vi import ZeroTtsVi  # noqa: E402
   36 from tts_vi_bench.measure import load_sentences  # noqa: E402
   37 from tts_vi_bench.metrics import corpus_cer, corpus_wer  # noqa: E402
   38 
   39 BENCH_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
   40 OUT_DIR = BENCH_ROOT / "results" / "seed-sensitivity"
   41 
   42 
   43 def main() -> int:
   44     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
   45     parser.add_argument("--engine", default="zerotts-vi",
   46                         choices=["zerotts-vi", "vieneu-vi"])
   47     parser.add_argument("--voice", default="baotrang")
   48     parser.add_argument("--seeds", default="11,22,33",
   49                         help="zerotts only: one pass per seed")
   50     parser.add_argument("--runs", type=int, default=None,
   51                         help="vieneu only: repeat N times (it has no seed knob)")
   52     parser.add_argument(
   53         "--sentences", type=Path,
   54         default=BENCH_ROOT / "data" / "sentences-conversational.jsonl")
   55     args = parser.parse_args()
   56 
   57     sentences = load_sentences(args.sentences)
   58     is_zero = args.engine == "zerotts-vi"
   59     # ZeroTTS varies by seed; VieNeu varies on its own, so its "passes" are bare
   60     # repeats and the label is the repeat index rather than a seed.
   61     passes = ([int(s) for s in args.seeds.split(",")] if is_zero
   62               else list(range(1, (args.runs or 6) + 1)))
   63 
   64     engine = ZeroTtsVi() if is_zero else VieNeuVi()
   65     engine.load()
   66     judge = PhoWhisperJudge()
   67     judge.load()
   68 
   69     rows = []
   70     for label in passes:
   71         wav_dir = OUT_DIR / args.engine / f"pass-{label}" / args.voice.replace(" ", "-")
   72         wav_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   73         hyps, refs = [], []
   74         for s in sentences:
   75             # Override the module-level seed for this pass only. Seeding here
   76             # rather than inside the engine keeps the engine's recorded
   77             # decode_params honest about what the MAIN run used.
   78             if is_zero:
   79                 np.random.seed(label)
   80                 samples = engine._tts.synthesize(s.text, voice=args.voice)
   81                 samples = np.asarray(samples, dtype=np.float32).reshape(-1)
   82             else:
   83                 samples, _sr = engine.synthesize(s.text, args.voice)
   84             path = wav_dir / f"{s.id}.wav"
   85             sf.write(path, samples, engine.sample_rate, subtype="PCM_16")
   86             hyps.append(judge.transcribe(path))
   87             refs.append(s.ref_text)
   88         wer, cer = corpus_wer(refs, hyps), corpus_cer(refs, hyps)
   89         rows.append({"pass": label, "engine": args.engine, "voice": args.voice,
   90                      "wer": wer, "cer": cer, "n": len(sentences)})
   91         kind = "seed" if is_zero else "repeat"
   92         print(f"{kind} {label}: WER {wer*100:.2f}%  CER {cer*100:.2f}%", flush=True)
   93 
   94     wers = [r["wer"] for r in rows]
   95     spread = max(wers) - min(wers)
   96     summary = {
   97         "engine": args.engine,
   98         "voice": args.voice,
   99         "sentence_set": args.sentences.stem,
  100         "judge": judge.judge_id,
  101         "runs": rows,
  102         "wer_spread_pp": spread * 100,
  103         "note": (
  104             "Compare wer_spread_pp against the ZeroTTS-minus-VieNeu gap in "
  105             "results/<tag>/intelligibility-summary.json. A spread of the same "
  106             "order as the gap means the gap is not a property of the engines "
  107             "and no verdict can rest on it."
  108         ),
  109     }
  110     OUT_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  111     (OUT_DIR / "summary.json").write_text(
  112         json.dumps(summary, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
  113     mean = sum(wers) / len(wers)
  114     print(f"\n{args.engine}/{args.voice}: mean WER {mean*100:.2f}%  "
  115           f"range {min(wers)*100:.2f}-{max(wers)*100:.2f}%  spread {spread*100:.2f}pp")
  116     print(f"[done] {OUT_DIR / 'summary.json'}")
  117     return 0
  118 
  119 
  120 if __name__ == "__main__":
  121     sys.exit(main())

   26 def _write(tmp_path, name, rows):
   27     path = tmp_path / name
   28     path.write_text(
   29         "# a policy header the loader must skip\n"
   30         + "\n".join(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) for r in rows)
   31         + "\n",
   32         encoding="utf-8",
   33     )
   34     return path
   35 
   36 
   37 def test_load_sentences_takes_the_declared_id(tmp_path):
   38     """The id comes from the row, never from its position.
   39 
   40     This is the divergence from the English harness's loader, and it is the
   41     whole reason two sentence sets can share one results tree: positional ids
   42     would make conversational and VIVOS rows collide on filename and overwrite
   43     each other's audio.
   44     """
   45     path = _write(
   46         tmp_path,
   47         "sentences-vivos.jsonl",
   48         [
   49             {"id": "vivos-VIVOSDEV01_R044", "text": "Một hai ba.", "ref_text": "MỘT HAI BA"},
   50             {"id": "vivos-VIVOSDEV02_R009", "text": "Bốn năm sáu.", "ref_text": "BỐN NĂM SÁU"},
   51         ],
   52     )
   53     got = load_sentences(path)
   54     assert [s.id for s in got] == ["vivos-VIVOSDEV01_R044", "vivos-VIVOSDEV02_R009"]
   55     assert got[0].ref_text == "MỘT HAI BA"
   56     assert got[0].text == "Một hai ba."
   57 
   58 
   59 def test_load_sentences_carries_tags(tmp_path):
   60     path = _write(
   61         tmp_path,
   62         "sentences-conversational.jsonl",
   63         [{"id": "conv-001", "text": "Xin chào.", "ref_text": "Xin chào.",
   64           "tags": ["short", "code-switch"]}],
   65     )
   66     assert load_sentences(path)[0].tags == ("short", "code-switch")
   67 
   68 
   69 def test_load_sentences_rejects_duplicate_ids(tmp_path):
   70     path = _write(
   71         tmp_path,
   72         "sentences-dup.jsonl",
   73         [
   74             {"id": "conv-001", "text": "A.", "ref_text": "A."},

   76         ],
   77     )

benchmarks/tts-vi/tests/test_measure_stream.py
    1 """Coverage for the underrun metric, which the TTFA conclusion rests on.
    2 
    3 `measure_stream` decides whether a fast first chunk is a real win or a stall
    4 dressed as one. A sign error or an off-by-one in where `total_samples` is read
    5 would flip the central comparison in the report, so it is pinned here against a
    6 fake engine whose chunk timing is controlled exactly.
    7 """
    8 
    9 import time
   10 
   11 import numpy as np
   12 import pytest
   13 
   14 from tts_vi_bench.run_engine import measure_stream
   15 
   16 SR = 48000
   17 
   18 
   19 class FakeStreamingEngine:
   20     """Yields chunks of a stated audio length, each after a stated wall delay."""
   21 
   22     def __init__(self, schedule: list[tuple[float, float]]):
   23         # (seconds to generate this chunk, seconds of audio it carries)
   24         self._schedule = schedule
   25         self.sample_rate = SR
   26 
   27     def synthesize_stream(self, text: str, voice: str):
   28         for gen_s, audio_s in self._schedule:
   29             time.sleep(gen_s)
   30             yield np.zeros(int(audio_s * SR), dtype=np.float32)
   31 
   32 
   33 def test_a_stream_that_stays_ahead_reports_a_negative_margin():
   34     """Each chunk generated far faster than the audio it carries: no underrun."""
   35     engine = FakeStreamingEngine([(0.01, 0.5)] * 4)
   36     out = measure_stream(engine, "x", "v")
   37     assert out["stream_chunks"] == 4
   38     assert out["stream_underrun_margin_s"] < 0, "a stream running ahead must be negative"
   39     assert out["stream_audio_s"] == pytest.approx(2.0, abs=0.01)
   40 
   41 
   42 def test_a_stream_that_falls_behind_reports_a_positive_margin():
   43     """First chunk is tiny, the next takes far longer than it can cover."""
   44     engine = FakeStreamingEngine([(0.01, 0.05), (0.40, 0.05), (0.01, 0.5)])
   45     out = measure_stream(engine, "x", "v")
   46     # After chunk 1 the listener has 50 ms of audio; chunk 2 arrives ~400 ms
   47     # later, so playback ran dry for roughly 350 ms.
   48     assert out["stream_underrun_margin_s"] > 0.2
   49 
   50 
   51 def test_ttfa_is_the_first_chunk_not_the_whole_stream():
   52     engine = FakeStreamingEngine([(0.05, 0.5), (0.30, 0.5)])
   53     out = measure_stream(engine, "x", "v")
   54     assert out["stream_ttfa_s"] == pytest.approx(0.05, abs=0.04)
   55     assert out["stream_total_s"] > out["stream_ttfa_s"]
   56 
   57 
   58 def test_the_margin_is_measured_from_first_audio_not_from_the_call():
   59     """A slow first chunk must not be charged again as an underrun.
   60 
   61     Playback starts when the listener first hears something. Measuring the
   62     margin from t0 would count the time-to-first-audio twice and report a stall
   63     on a stream that never stalled — the specific error this guards.
   64     """
   65     engine = FakeStreamingEngine([(0.30, 0.5), (0.01, 0.5)])
   66     out = measure_stream(engine, "x", "v")
   67     assert out["stream_ttfa_s"] == pytest.approx(0.30, abs=0.05)
   68     assert out["stream_underrun_margin_s"] < 0, (
   69         "the 300 ms first chunk is TTFA, not a shortfall"
   70     )
   71 
   72 
   73 def test_empty_chunks_do_not_start_the_clock():
   74     engine = FakeStreamingEngine([(0.02, 0.0), (0.02, 0.5)])
   75     out = measure_stream(engine, "x", "v")
   76     assert out["stream_chunks"] == 1, "a zero-length chunk is not audio"
   77     assert out["stream_ttfa_s"] >= 0.03
   78 
   79 
   80 def test_a_single_chunk_stream_has_no_margin_to_report():
   81     engine = FakeStreamingEngine([(0.02, 0.5)])
   82     out = measure_stream(engine, "x", "v")
   83     assert out["stream_chunks"] == 1
   84     assert out["stream_underrun_margin_s"] is None
benchmarks/tts-vi/tests/test_metrics.py
    1 """Metrics coverage, including the bootstrap that replaces the r1/r2 rule for WER."""
    2 
    3 import pytest
    4 
    5 from tts_vi_bench.metrics import (
    6     corpus_cer,
    7     corpus_wer,
    8     paired_bootstrap_wer,
    9     sentence_errors,
   10     win_loss_tie,
   11 )
   12 from tts_vi_bench.text_normalize import normalize_text
   13 
   14 
   15 def test_normalizer_preserves_vietnamese_diacritics():
   16     """Casing and punctuation go; tone marks must not."""
   17     assert normalize_text("Xin chào, các bạn!") == "xin chào các bạn"
   18     assert normalize_text("ĐƯỜNG") == "đường"
   19 
   20 
   21 def test_normalizer_composes_equivalent_unicode_forms():
   22     """NFC, so a decomposed and a composed 'ế' score as the same word."""
   23     assert normalize_text("ế") == normalize_text("ế")
   24 
   25 
   26 def test_perfect_transcript_scores_zero():
   27     assert corpus_wer(["xin chào"], ["xin chào"]) == 0.0
   28     assert corpus_cer(["xin chào"], ["xin chào"]) == 0.0
   29 
   30 
   31 def test_one_substitution_in_four_words():
   32     assert corpus_wer(["một hai ba bốn"], ["một hai ba năm"]) == pytest.approx(0.25)
   33 
   34 
   35 def test_cer_separates_a_near_miss_from_a_miss():
   36     """The reason CER is reported beside WER on Vietnamese.
   37 
   38     Both hypotheses lose the same single word to WER; only CER shows that one
   39     missed by a tone mark and the other by a different word entirely.
   40     """
   41     assert corpus_wer(["cho tôi nước"], ["cho tôi nướt"]) == pytest.approx(1 / 3)
   42     assert corpus_wer(["cho tôi nước"], ["cho tôi bánh"]) == pytest.approx(1 / 3)
   43     assert corpus_cer(["cho tôi nước"], ["cho tôi nướt"]) < corpus_cer(
   44         ["cho tôi nước"], ["cho tôi bánh"]
   45     )
   46 
   47 
   48 def test_ref_hyp_length_mismatch_is_an_error():
   49     with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="mismatch"):
   50         corpus_wer(["a", "b"], ["a"])
   51 
   52 
   53 def test_sentence_errors_counts_edits_and_reference_length():
   54     edits, n_ref = sentence_errors("một hai ba bốn", "một hai ba năm")
   55     assert (edits, n_ref) == (1, 4)
   56 
   57 
   58 def test_bootstrap_reports_a_tie_when_systems_are_identical():
   59     refs = [f"câu số {i} ở đây" for i in range(30)]
   60     out = paired_bootstrap_wer(refs, refs, refs, n_resamples=300)
   61     assert out["observed_diff"] == pytest.approx(0.0)
   62     assert not out["separates"], "identical systems must not separate"
   63 
   64 
   65 def test_bootstrap_separates_a_clearly_better_system():
   66     refs = [f"một hai ba bốn năm câu {i}" for i in range(40)]
   67     good = list(refs)  # perfect
   68     bad = ["hoàn toàn sai khác biệt lắm rồi" for _ in refs]
   69     out = paired_bootstrap_wer(refs, good, bad, n_resamples=300)
   70     assert out["observed_diff"] < 0, "A is better, so the difference is negative"
   71     assert out["separates"]
   72     assert out["p_a_better"] == pytest.approx(1.0)
   73 
   74 
   75 def test_win_loss_tie_counts_per_sentence_outcomes():
   76     refs = ["một hai", "ba bốn"]
   77     a = ["một hai", "sai hết"]
   78     b = ["sai rồi", "ba bốn"]
   79     assert win_loss_tie(refs, a, b) == {"a_wins": 1, "b_wins": 1, "ties": 0}

benchmarks/tts-vi/data/sentences-conversational.jsonl
    1 # Conversational Vietnamese — the register the app actually emits.
    2 #
    3 # These are the shape of Gemini's Vietnamese translations of conversational
    4 # English: asking prices, ordering, directions, booking, apologising. Every row
    5 # carries at least one clause boundary where it is natural, because the TTFA
    6 # arms split on the app's own rule and a set of boundary-free sentences would
    7 # measure clause splitting doing nothing.
    8 #
    9 # ORTHOGRAPHY POLICY — load-bearing, enforced by the build check:
   10 #   `text` spells every numeral, time and currency amount out in Vietnamese
   11 #   words and contains no abbreviations. The round-trip scores an ASR transcript
   12 #   against this string, and the shared normalizer leaves numbers as written by
   13 #   design, so a digit here would cost word errors for a rendering that was in
   14 #   fact correct. benchmarks/stt/README.md documents the failure with a worked
   15 #   example: a hypothesis correctly saying "17:00" scores three word errors
   16 #   against a reference reading "MƯỜI BẢY GIỜ". Keeping digits out keeps this
   17 #   metric about the audio.
   18 #
   19 # CODE-SWITCH SUBSET — rows tagged `code-switch` carry embedded English, which
   20 #   is both what ZeroTTS specifically claims and what real translated output
   21 #   contains. KNOWN SCORING HAZARD: the normalizer splits `check-in` into two
   22 #   tokens, so a transcript of `checkin` costs word errors regardless of how
   23 #   intelligible the audio was. Over nine rows that is not a rate worth quoting,
   24 #   so phase 4 reports CER as primary here and hand-reviews every hypothesis.
   25 #
   26 # PROVENANCE: authored for this benchmark, not drawn from a corpus, so `ref_text`
   27 #   is the authored string rather than a human transcription. This is an
   28 #   in-domain, author-chosen set and NOT an adversarial one — and its author is
   29 #   also the person judging the outcome, which is a bias worth stating on the
   30 #   artifact rather than only in the report.

 if p.name=='intelligibility.jsonl':continue
 print('\\n'+str(p))

import json,pathlib,re,unicodedata,collections
p=pathlib.Path('benchmarks/tts-vi/results/r2/intelligibility.jsonl')
def norm(s):return re.sub(r'\\s+',' ',re.sub(r'["'^'"\\w\\s]|_',' ',unicodedata.normalize('NFC',s).lower())).strip()
def lev(a,b):
 prev=list(range(len(b)+1))
 for i,x in enumerate(a,1):
  cur=[i]
  for j,y in enumerate(b,1):cur.append(min(cur[-1]+1,prev[j]+1,prev[j-1]+(x"'!=y)))
  prev=cur
 return prev[-1]
groups=collections.defaultdict(list)
for i,line in enumerate(p.read_text().splitlines(),1):
 r=json.loads(line)
 if r['"'type']=='header':print('HEADER',r);continue

 ref=norm(r['reference']); h1=norm(r['hyp_phowhisper-small']);h2=norm(r['hyp_zipformer-vi'])
 e1=lev(ref.split(),h1.split()); e2=lev(ref.split(),h2.split())

  print(i,r['sentence_id'],r['engine'],r['voice_slug'],'edits',e1,e2,'REF',r['reference'],'PHO',r['hyp_phowhisper-small'],'ZIP',r['hyp_zipformer-vi'])
for key,rs in groups.items():
 print('AGGREGATE',key,'n',len(rs))

  for subset in ['all','code-switch','non-code-switch']:
   ss=[r for r in rs if subset=='all' or ((subset=='code-switch')==('code-switch'in r['tags']))]
   if not ss:continue
   refs=[norm(r['reference']) for r in ss];hyps=[norm(r['hyp_'+judge]) for r in ss]
   we=sum(lev(r.split(),h.split()) for r,h in zip(refs,hyps));nw=sum(len(r.split())for r in refs)
   ce=sum(lev(r,h)for r,h in zip(refs,hyps));nc=sum(map(len,refs))
   print(judge,subset,len(ss),'word edits',we,'words',nw,'WER',round(we/nw*100,4),'CER',round(ce/nc*100,4))
